# Web assets the AM-Bench scenes load at run time, baked into the image (images/ambench/Dockerfile): sha256, URL, path in the
# image. The image mirrors the asset buckets' layouts under /opt/isaac-assets (production) and /opt/isaac-assets/staging
# (NVIDIA's staging bucket, where the SimReady lemon, lime and table of LemonHarvesting live). The tooling points Isaac's
# asset root (/persistent/isaac/asset_root/cloud) at /opt/isaac-assets/Assets/Isaac/5.1, and localize_assets.py rewrites the
# URLs in AM-Bench's USDs to these copies. Everything else is found by relative path inside the mirror.
# Generated by walking every USD the twelve tasks load (and every MDL's textures), recursively: Isaac Lab's default ground,
# the wall's material, the objects' payloads, materials and textures, the robot's paint, and NDT's warehouse scene
# (Isaac/Samples/Replicator/Benchmark/full_warehouse_worker_benchmark_sdg.usd with its props, characters and materials).
